Motivation: 先前工作建立了单层Transformer与最近邻分类器在二分类场景下的等价性，但其多分类结果依赖非标准的舍入（rounding-based）方法，而非实践中普遍使用的argmax分类头。这一差距导致理论结果与实际应用存在不一致，需要弥合。

Method: 通过引入单纯形编码（simplex encoding）表示多分类标签，作者分析了带argmax分类头的单层Transformer的决策行为，并严格证明其与一最近邻（1-nearest-neighbor）分类器在多分类设置下的行为等价。

Result: 证明了在多分类设置下，采用argmax分类头的单层Transformer与一最近邻分类器完全等价，从而填补了先前研究在多分类情形下依赖非标准舍入方法的理论空白。

Conclusion: 该结果进一步巩固了Transformer与最近邻模型之间的理论联系，使多分类场景下的等价性建立在与实际应用一致的argmax分类头之上，为理解Transformer的归纳偏置提供了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We extend recent work establishing an equivalence between one-layer transformers and nearest-neighbor classifiers in the binary setting to the multiclass case. By leveraging the simplex encoding, we show that one-layer transformers with an argmax classification head behave identically to a one-nearest-neighbor classifier in the multiclass setting. This closes a gap left by prior work, whose multiclass result relied on a non-standard rounding-based approach rather than the typical argmax head used in practice.

</details>


### [91] [Bandits in Prod: Hyperparameter Optimization at Inference Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01335)
*Louis Abraham,Tuan-Anh Nguyen,Nicolas Devatine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在线超参数优化（OHPO）问题，将其形式化为无限臂老虎机问题，并引入IMABO通用框架及IMOSS策略，在多种场景（从经典机器学习模型到LLM智能体配置）中取得最优累积遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多生产系统只能在真实请求上评估配置并获得噪声反馈（如现代智能体系统中的推理时选择：模型选择、检索深度、提示策略、解码温度等），且通常没有代表性的验证数据。这需要一个在线的、无需离线验证集的超参数优化框架。

Method: 将OHPO形式化为混合和条件搜索空间上的无限臂老虎机问题。提出IMABO框架（任意bandit策略×任意提议oracle的组合）及IMOSS策略（活动集以t^β速率增长的无重启任何时候策略），并与三种oracle结合：Tree-structured Parzen Estimator、逐坐标bandit驱动的变异oracle、以及预训练的表格型基础模型。

Result: 推导出期望累积分位数遗憾界O(p_ρ^{-1/β} + T^{(1+β)/2})。三种oracle均优于均匀随机基线proposal，IMABO在多种OHPO设置（从经典机器学习模型调优到LLM智能体配置）中取得最低累积遗憾。

Conclusion: IMABO为生产环境中无验证数据的在线配置调优提供了统一的、理论上保证的高效框架，在经典机器学习与LLM智能体等异构场景中均表现出优越性。

Abstract: Many production systems can assess a configuration only by using it on live requests and observing noisy feedback. Modern agentic systems are a prominent example, with inference-time choices such as model selection, retrieval depth, prompting strategy, and decoding temperature, yet often with no representative validation data. We formalize this setting as Online Hyperparameter Optimization (OHPO) and cast it as an infinitely many-armed bandit over mixed and conditional search spaces. We introduce IMABO, a general framework that combines any bandit policy for choosing among already sampled configurations with any oracle for proposing new ones. We instantiate it with IMOSS, a restart-free anytime policy whose active set grows as $t^β$, and prove an expected cumulative quantile-regret bound of $O(p_ρ^{-1/β} + T^{(1+β)/2})$, where $β\in(0,1)$ controls active-set growth and $p_ρ$ lower-bounds the probability that a proposed configuration falls in the top-$ρ$ fraction of the search space. We combine IMOSS with three practical oracles: a Tree-structured Parzen Estimator, an incumbent-mutation oracle driven by a per-coordinate bandit, and a pretrained tabular foundation model, all three improving over the uniform random oracle baseline. IMABO obtains the lowest cumulative regret across diverse OHPO settings, from tuning classical machine-learning models to configuring LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [92] [SMELT: Scaling Laws for Compute-Matched MoE Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01343)
*Shaowen Wang,Ge Zhang,Kairong Luo,Yuhao Wu,Shaofan Liu,Jiaheng Liu,Wenhao Huang,Shen Yan,Jian Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SMELT配方，在严格匹配每token FLOPs、总非嵌入参数和KV缓存三种预算的条件下，将MoE Transformer中间一半层循环两次。通过对比基线，SMELT在四个规模（最大54B参数）上的损失下降更快，在计算最优前沿节省6.8%-18.0%的训练FLOPs，且下游基准优势超出验证损失预测，代码任务上效果最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer通过迭代共享层块来增加有效深度，但大多数评估在固定模型大小下比较，混淆了架构优势与额外FLOPs。作者希望在不增加计算预算的前提下，公平地验证循环架构是否真正优于非循环基线。

Method: 在MoE Transformer上进行循环实验，通过一系列消融研究，提出SMELT配方：对中间一半层循环两次，同时匹配未循环基线的每token FLOPs、总非嵌入参数和KV缓存三项预算。在四个规模上缩放模型，并为每种架构分别拟合Chinchilla式缩放定律。

Result: SMELT在计算最优前沿上节省6.8%-18.0%的训练FLOPs；优势向下游基准转移且超过验证损失预测，在Code任务上最强，并随序列长度和few-shot示例数量增长。机制分析显示第二次循环访问减少了注意力汇，将注意力质量转向内容相关token。

Conclusion: 循环架构在严格匹配预算的条件下仍能提升Transformer性能，深度复用可以转化为可测量的收益，为实际应用提供了可行配方。

Abstract: Looped Transformers increase effective depth by iterating a shared block of layers, but most evaluations compare at fixed model size, conflating architectural advantage with extra FLOPs. We study looping on Mixture-of-Experts Transformers while closely matching per-token FLOPs, total non-embedding parameters, and KV cache. Through a series of ablations, we arrive at a recipe we call SMELT (Sparse MoE Transformer, middle layers Loop Twice), which loops the middle half of layers twice while matching the unlooped Baseline on all three budgets. We scale SMELT across four sizes up to 54B non-embedding parameters and fit a separate Chinchilla-style scaling law for each architecture. SMELT's loss drops faster with compute, saving 6.8--18.0\% of training FLOPs on the compute-optimal frontier. The advantage transfers to downstream benchmarks beyond what validation loss predicts, is largest on Code, and grows with sample length and the number of in-context examples. Mechanistic analysis shows that the second visit reduces the attention sink and redirects mass toward content-relevant tokens, an inductive bias that may underlie the observed performance gains. These results show that looping can improve Transformers even under budget matching, offering a practical recipe that turns depth reuse into measurable gains.

</details>


### [93] [Contribution-Aware Bandwidth Allocation for Multimodal Split Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01406)
*Iason Ofeidis,Leandros Tassiulas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ModalShare，一种用于多模态Split Learning的跨模态分配机制。在固定上行带宽预算下，ModalShare通过Shapley贡献分数决定各模态的压缩保留比例，取代现有"各模态等比例压缩"的做法，在相同负载下显著提升多模态融合预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型越来越多地部署在网络边缘，但在数据中心训练。边缘客户端持有多个传感器流，无法为每个模态各托管一个编码器。Split Learning允许仅将前几层放在设备上训练，但上行链路每一轮都需要传输每个模态的smashed activations。现有压缩方案给每个模态相同的keep-ratio，即共享预算按smashed-activation维度成比例分配，这与其对各模态对融合预测的贡献无关。因此需要一种显式的模态间分配决策。

Method: ModalShare将上行预算的分配视为显式决策——跨模态分配（inter-modality allocation）。在固定上行预算下，每个策略传输相同的期望负载，仅在各模态间如何拆分上有别。分配器由服务器基于已收到的激活coalition计算每个模态的Shapley贡献分数，据此设定各模态的keep-ratio。该分数计算不增加上行流量、不需客户端计算，也无需预先知道哪个流属于哪个模态。

Result: 在5倍压缩下，ModalShare相比等keep-ratio方案在CREMA-D上将准确率提升15.4个百分点，在MVSA上提升12.4个百分点（负载匹配条件下）。在三种压缩器、三个数据集、四种预算配置下均表现稳定。实验结果还表明现有压缩器在多模态场景下表现欠佳，而ModalShare能够恢复其遗留的性能增益。

Conclusion: 模态间分配是多模态Split Learning中一个此前被忽视但至关重要的维度。现有压缩器在多模态场景下性能受限，通过将预算分配建立在对各模态实际贡献的度量子上，ModalShare能够在保持上行负载不变的前提下显著提升多模态训练精度。

Abstract: Multimodal models are increasingly the default option for perception at the network edge, yet they are trained almost entirely in the datacenter, because a client holding several sensor streams cannot host an encoder per modality. Split Learning makes such training feasible by keeping only the first layers on the device, at the cost of an uplink that must carry smashed activations for every modality at every step. Existing compression schemes give each modality the same keep-ratio, so the shared budget is divided in proportion to smashed-activation dimension, a quantity unrelated to how much each modality contributes to the fused prediction. We make that division an explicit decision and call it inter-modality allocation: under a fixed uplink budget, every policy transmits the same expected payload and differs only in how that payload is split across modalities. Our allocator, ModalShare, sets each modality's keep-ratio from a Shapley contribution score that the server computes over coalitions of activations it has already received. Measuring this score adds no uplink traffic and no client-side computation, and needs no prior knowledge of which stream is which. ModalShare improves accuracy over equal keep-ratios by 15.4 and 12.4 percentage points on CREMA-D and MVSA at matched payload in 5x compression, with strong performance across three compressors, three datasets, and four budgets. We show that existing compressors underperform in multimodal settings, with ModalShare recovering what gains are left behind.

</details>


### [94] [Predicting Subsurface Abnormalities Growth using Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01417)
*Mehrdad Shafiei Dizaji,Hoda Azari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将物理信息神经网络（PINNs）集成到探地雷达（GPR）数据预测中的创新框架。该模型由CNN、空间特征通道注意力（SFCA）机制、ConvLSTM以及时间特征帧注意力（TFFA）模块组成，通过将电磁波传播的物理定律嵌入深度学习架构中，提高了GPR数据预测的准确性，对桥梁面板状况评估和土木基础设施的非破坏性评估（NDE）具有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的GPR数据预测方法未能充分利用物理规律，而纯深度学习模型缺乏物理约束可能导致预测结果不符合电磁波传播的基本原理。作者希望通过将物理知识融入神经网络，类比医学影像预测肿瘤行为的方式，提高GPR数据预测的物理一致性和精度，以支持基础设施劣化评估。

Method: 提出一个包含三个组件的专用PINN模型：（1）CNN用于提取视觉/空间特征；（2）空间特征通道注意力（SFCA）机制通过自适应计算通道注意力权重来精调视觉特征响应；（3）ConvLSTM用于捕获时序特征；（4）时间特征帧注意力（TFFA）模块通过自适应的帧注意力权重提取最相关的时序特征。整个架构将电磁波传播的物理定律嵌入网络训练过程中，使预测遵循基本的物理原理。

Result: 实验表明该物理信息神经网络模型在GPR数据预测方面展现出增强的准确性，验证了将物理约束直接集成到神经网络中的有效性，对桥梁面板状况评估和其他土木基础设施评价具有良好的应用效果。

Conclusion: PINNs有望变革非破坏性评估（NDE）领域，通过物理基础的建模提高基础设施劣化预测的精度，并为理解劣化的基本机制提供更深层次的物理洞察。

Abstract: The research explores the pioneering integration of Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) into the domain of Ground-Penetrating Radar (GPR) data prediction. This research presents a detailed development framework for a specialized PINN model, proficient at interpreting and forecasting GPR data, much like how medical imaging models predict tumor behavior. By harnessing the synergy between deep learning algorithms and the physical laws governing subsurface structures or in medical terms, human tissues the model effectively embeds the physics of electromagnetic wave propagation into its architecture. This ensures that predictions not only align with fundamental physical principles but also mirror the precision needed in medical diagnostics for detecting and monitoring tumors. The suggested deep learning structure comprises three components: a CNN, a spatial feature channel attention (SFCA) mechanism, and ConvLSTM, along with temporal feature frame attention (TFFA) modules. The attention mechanism computes channel attention and temporal attention weights using self-adaptation, thereby fine tuning the visual and temporal feature responses to extract the most pertinent and significant visual and temporal features. By integrating physics directly into the neural network, our model has shown enhanced accuracy in forecasting GPR data. This improvement is vital for conducting effective assessments of bridge deck conditions and other evaluations related to civil infrastructure. The use of Physics Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) has demonstrated the potential to transform the field of Non-Destructive Evaluation (NDE) by enhancing the precision of infrastructure deterioration predictions. Moreover, it offers a deeper insight into the fundamental mechanisms of deterioration, viewed through the prism of physics-based models.

</details>


### [95] [Provably Safe Sim-to-Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01418)
*Tingting Ni,Maryam Kamgarpour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了安全模拟到现实迁移问题，在无奖励安全强化学习框架下提出了一种计算高效算法，利用模拟器信息显著降低真实世界交互次数，同时保证安全探索并为任意潜在奖励函数计算近似最优可行策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界强化学习的样本复杂度很高，常见做法是先在模拟器中训练策略再部署到真实世界，但模拟器与真实系统之间的失配可能导致策略次优。修正这种失配需要收集真实数据，而许多应用（如机器人和医疗）中数据收集本身受安全约束限制。因此需要解决如何在利用不完美模拟器的同时保证安全真实数据收集和得到近似最优可行策略的问题。

Method: 将安全模拟到现实迁移问题形式化为无奖励安全强化学习框架下的问题，设计了一个计算高效的算法，该算法利用模拟器信息来可证明地减少真实世界交互，同时确保安全探索，并能为任意潜在奖励函数计算近似最优可行策略。

Result: 给出了真实世界样本复杂度界，该界以模拟器与真实系统之间的失配程度为参数，刻画了使用模拟器带来的收益。

Conclusion: 提出的算法以可证明的方式实现了安全模拟到现实迁移：既能充分利用模拟器降低真实世界交互成本，又能保证安全性并为任意奖励函数求得近似最优策略，其收益程度与模拟器失配程度相关。

Abstract: To mitigate the sample complexity of real-world reinforcement learning (RL), a common practice is to first train a policy in a simulator, where samples are cheap, and then deploy the learned policy in the real world with the hope that it generalizes effectively. Such direct sim-to-real transfer is not guaranteed to succeed: simulator-trained policies can be suboptimal in the real world due to sim-to-real mismatch. Correcting this mismatch requires collecting data from the real system, but in many applications, such as robotics and healthcare, this data-collection process is itself subject to safety constraints. This gives rise to the problem of safe sim-to-real transfer: how can an agent exploit an imperfect simulator while ensuring safe real-world data collection and learning a near-optimal feasible policy for the target system? We address this problem by formulating safe sim-to-real transfer within the framework of reward-free safe RL. We design a computationally efficient algorithm that exploits simulator information to provably reduce real-world interaction while ensuring safe exploration and enabling the computation of a near-optimal feasible policy for any potential reward function. Our real-world sample complexity bound characterizes the benefit of using the simulator in terms of the sim-to-real mismatch.

</details>


### [96] [CATeye: Coupled Attribute-Topology Invariance Learning for Voucher Abuse Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01425)
*Tian Tian,Shuaicheng Niu,Hao Kuang,Yuanhang Hu,Dong Li,Zhiqi Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CATeye框架，通过属性选择器(AIS)和图拓扑选择器(EIS)解耦属性-拓扑耦合分布偏移，在多视图学习中分离不变与易变分量，显著提升电商平台优惠券滥用检测的跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商优惠券滥用检测面临严重的分布偏移问题：欺诈模式随时间与地域快速演变，导致现有检测模型性能严重退化且需频繁重训。核心难点在于属性与拓扑的耦合偏移——属性邻近性构建的图边使得环境驱动的属性偏移会传导至拓扑结构，图神经网络消息传递进一步放大变异信号。

Method: 提出耦合属性-拓扑不变性学习框架CATeye：(1)属性不变性选择器(AIS)学习节点自适应掩码以过滤非不变属性；(2)基于保留的不变属性，边不变性选择器(EIS)采样不变子图并隔离非不变边；(3)利用所得不变与非不变分量构造多视图，应用视图特定目标函数强调域不变表示、抑制域特定变化。

Result: 在Lazada（东南亚大型电商平台）专有数据集及公开基准上，CATeye一致优于9个强域泛化与图异常检测基线，相较于最强基线平均F1分数最高提升8.61%。源代码已公开。

Conclusion: 通过解耦属性与拓扑的耦合偏移并利用多视图学习强化不变性，CATeye有效提升了优惠券滥用检测在跨时间、跨区域的域泛化能力，验证了关注耦合偏移视角对图域泛化问题的重要性。

Abstract: Voucher abuse poses a major challenge in e-commerce, where malicious users exploit promotional vouchers for profit. Unfortunately, fraud patterns evolve rapidly over time and across regions, causing distribution shifts that degrade existing detection models unless retrained frequently. To tackle this, we propose the Coupled Attribute-Topology Invariance Learning framework (CATeye). The key challenge arises from coupled attribute-topology shift, where edges built from attribute proximity cause environment-driven attribute shift to induce shifted topology, thereby amplifying variant signals through GNN message passing. CATeye sees through such coupled shifts with two learnable selectors. First, an Attribute Invariance Selector (AIS) learns node-adaptive masks to filter out non-invariant attributes. Then, conditioned on retained invariant attributes, an Edge Invariance Selector (EIS) samples an invariant subgraph and isolates non-invariant edges. Using the resulting invariant and non-invariant components, CATeye constructs multiple views and applies view-specific objectives to emphasize domain-invariant representations while suppressing domain-specific variations. Experiments on both a proprietary dataset from Lazada, a major Southeast Asian e-commerce platform, and a public benchmark show that CATeye consistently outperforms nine strong domain generalization and graph anomaly detection baselines, achieving up to an 8.61% improvement in average F1 score over the strongest baseline. Source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Tian0426/CATeye.

</details>


### [97] [TRIAGE: Three-level Routing and Intelligent Agent Guidance for Efficient Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01428)
*Ruocan Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TRIAGE，一个基于历史轨迹复用的三层路由框架，通过TaaS（Trajectory-as-a-Skill）机制将历史执行轨迹抽象为可复用技能，实现"经验即服务"。在安全监控和ToolBench数据集上分别实现62.3%和76.3%的token节省，并验证了冷启动到成熟的在线学习演化过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReAct范式的LLM智能体存在根本性效率问题：每次查询都从头开始完整推理循环，相似查询重复执行相同步骤，没有利用历史经验，导致大量token浪费。

Method: 提出三层路由框架TRIAGE：Level 1直接复用（完全相同的查询，0 token）；Level 2技能替换（相似查询通过确定性参数替换执行，0 token）；Level 3完整ReAct（新颖查询自动存储供未来复用）。核心创新TaaS将历史执行轨迹抽象为可复用技能，并设计了自动技能提取机制，从高频轨迹模式中提炼确定性技能，形成正反馈循环。

Result: 在1,007个安全监控查询中实现62.3% token节省，其中Level 2占56.0%、Level 1占5.5%，均零成本执行。跨域验证（ToolBench 15领域345查询）实现76.3% token减少。在线学习实验中，Level 2命中率在前100个查询内从0%升至57%，平均token成本从198降至74.7。

Conclusion: TRIAGE验证了通过历史轨迹复用可以显著降低LLM智能体的token消耗，语义路由具有跨领域泛化能力，且系统具有"越用越高效"的自增强特性。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents based on the ReAct paradigm have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in tool use and task execution. However, ReAct suffers from a fundamental efficiency problem: every query triggers a complete reasoning loop from scratch, and similar queries repeat identical steps without leveraging historical experience. We propose TRIAGE,a three-level routing framework that reduces token consumption by reusing historical execution trajectories. Its core innovation is TaaS (Trajectory-as-a-Skill), which abstracts historical execution trajectories into reusable skills, realizing 'experience as a service'. TRIAGE classifies queries into three levels: (1) Direct Reuse-identical queries, 0 tokens; (2) Skill Substitution-similar queries, 0 tokens via deterministic parameter substitution; (3) Full ReAct-novel queries, automatically stored for future reuse. In large-scale experiments on 1,007 security monitoring queries, TRIAGE achieves 62.3% token savings, with 56.0% of queries at Level 2 and 5.5% at Level 1, both executing at zero cost. Cross-domain validation on ToolBench (15 domains, 345 queries) achieves 76.3% token reduction, confirming the generalizability of semantic routing. An online learning experiment demonstrates cold-start-to-mature evolution: the L2 hit rate rises from 0% to 57% within the first 100 queries, and the average token cost drops from 198 to 74.7. We also propose an automatic Skill extraction mechanism that distills high-frequency trajectory patterns into deterministic Skills, creating a positive feedback loop of 'the more you use it, the more efficient it becomes'.

</details>


### [98] [Learning Sparse Decision Trees via Transformer Variational Auto-Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01430)
*Giacomo Fidone,Alessio Cascione,Riccardo Guidotti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TREVIS，一种基于树Transformer变分自编码器（TTVAE）的决策树学习方法，通过将决策树映射到潜在连续空间，实现梯度优化的多个目标联合学习，在保持预测性能的同时提升结构稀疏性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策树被广泛使用但现有学习算法主要关注预测性能，忽略了结构稀疏性等其他理想属性，缺乏对复杂多目标联合优化的有效方法。由于决策树具有离散搜索空间，难以直接进行梯度优化。

Method: TREVIS采用树Transformer变分自编码器（TTVAE），将决策树编码到潜在空间，将离散搜索问题转化为连续优化问题，通过可微分的代理模型（surrogate model）进行梯度优化，以联合优化预测性能和稀疏性等多个目标。

Result: 实验表明，TREVIS学习的决策树在预测性能上与现有近最优算法相当，同时在结构稀疏性上优于这些方法。

Conclusion: TREVIS证明了通过潜在空间连续化能够有效实现对决策树复杂多目标的联合优化，在保持预测准确性的前提下获得更稀疏的树结构，为决策树学习提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Decision trees are among the most widely used models in machine learning, largely due to their transparent decision logic, making them well-suited for high-stakes decision-making contexts. However, most existing learning algorithms focus on predictive performance, overlooking the joint optimization of other desirable properties, such as structural sparsity. In this work we propose TREVIS, an approach for learning decision trees with respect to complex objectives, based on the exploration of the latent space of a Tree Transformer Variational Auto-Encoder (TTVAE). By mapping decision trees onto latent representations, TREVIS replaces the discrete search space with a continuous one, enabling gradient-based optimization via a differentiable surrogate model. We experiment with TREVIS for learning decision trees that jointly optimize predictive performance and sparsity. Results show that TREVIS discovers decision trees matching the predictive performance of existing near-optimal algorithms while improving their structural sparsity.

</details>


### [99] [Efficiently Estimating Optimal Hyperparameter Scaling Laws through Power-Law Entropy Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01431)
*Zhiliang Chen,Sebastian Ament,David Eriksson,Maximilian Balandat,Eytan Bakshy,Jihao Andreas Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Power-Law Entropy Search (PLES)，一种基于多保真贝叶斯优化的计算成本感知采集函数，用于高效估计大规模语言模型（LLM）训练的最优超参数缩放律。PLES通过选择能最大化降低缩放律估计不确定性的候选配置（按单位计算成本衡量），在不到传统网格搜索十分之一的计算预算内即可收敛到准确的缩放律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超参数缩放律的准确估计对于在工业规模下预测最优训练配置至关重要，但传统方法需要对大量训练运行进行穷举式网格搜索，消耗巨大的计算资源，缺乏效率。

Method: PLES构建在多保真贝叶斯优化框架上，核心创新在于其采集函数不以优化单一目标函数为导向，而是搜索能够最大程度降低整个缩放律估计不确定性的候选配置；每次迭代选择单位计算成本下不确定性降低最大的候选，从而自然倾向于信息量大的小规模实验。

Result: 在合成基准、基于真实LLM训练数据的代理模型以及实际LLM预训练运行三种设置下，PLES均能以不到传统网格搜索和其他基线方法十分之一的计算预算，收敛到准确的最优超参数缩放律。

Conclusion: PLES作为一种计算成本感知的自适应实验方法，能够高效且准确地估计LLM训练的最优超参数缩放律，大幅降低了计算资源消耗，为大规模训练配置预测提供了实用可行的方案。

Abstract: Optimal hyperparameter scaling laws describe how the best hyperparameters for large language model (LLM) training change with model and data scale, enabling practitioners to predict optimal configurations at production scales without expensive large-scale tuning. However, estimating these scaling laws conventionally requires exhaustive grid searches over thousands of training runs, consuming enormous computational resources. We introduce Power-Law Entropy Search (PLES), a computational cost-aware acquisition function built on multi-fidelity Bayesian optimization that efficiently estimates optimal hyperparameter scaling laws through adaptive experimentation. A key innovation in PLES is that it searches for candidates that reduce the overall uncertainty of a scaling law estimate, instead of optimizing a single objective function. At each iteration, PLES selects the candidate configuration that maximally reduces the uncertainty of the scaling law estimates per unit computational cost, naturally favoring informative small-scale experiments. We evaluate PLES on synthetic benchmarks, surrogate models fitted to real LLM training data, and actual LLM pre-training runs. Across all settings, PLES converges to accurate optimal hyperparameter scaling laws using less than one-tenth of the computational budget required by conventional grid search and other baselines.

</details>


### [100] [Edge-Girth as a Structural Edge Feature for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01441)
*Lilian Marey,Charlotte Laclau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于"边围长+重数"(edge-girth and multiplicity)的逐边不变量描述符，可编码任意长度的最短环，注入门控消息传递架构EGAGNN后，在ZINC-12k上以104k参数达到比最强对照低三倍的MAE；同时证明在每条边看到相同最短环长度和数量的图上该描述符退化为常数，模型坍缩回1-WL界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消息传递GNN的表达能力被1-WL测试所限制，常见的补救方法是用固定小结构(如三角形、固定长度环)的计数来增强特征，但这种方式需要预先选择子结构大小，而这种选择通常盲目且无法适应数据。

Method: 提出边的围长(通过该边的最短环长度)及其重数(这样的最短环数量)作为逐边不变量，可通过每条边的单次广度优先搜索精确计算，能报告任意长度的环而不需预先设定大小。将该描述符注入门控消息传递架构形成EGAGNN模型。

Result: 在ZINC-12k回归基准上，EGAGNN以104k参数取得比最强门控对照低三倍的测试MAE；与同架构下有界环计数描述符相比，仅在计数高达长度8的字典达到相同性能(且需两倍通道数)，而截断到长度4的字典效果不优于无结构信息。在BREC基准的图判别上，所有90对满足退化条件的图中没有一对能被区分。

Conclusion: 边围长描述符无需预设环长度上限，有效增强GNN表达能力且具有计算上的简洁性；然而存在匹配的理论局限性：当所有边的最短环长度和重数一致时，描述符退化为常数，模型回到1-WL表达力边界。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNN) based on message passing are provably no more powerful than the one-dimensional Weisfeiler--Leman colour-refinement test (1-WL): two graphs it cannot tell apart receive identical representations, however deep or wide the network. A common remedy augments node or edge features with precomputed structural descriptors, most often counts of a fixed small subgraph such as triangles or longer cycles, but such counts require committing in advance to the size of the substructure counted, a choice usually made blind to the data. We study a descriptor that avoids this choice. The edge-girth of an edge is the length of a shortest cycle through it, and its multiplicity is the number of such shortest cycles; together they form a per-edge invariant that reports cycles of arbitrary length, computable exactly by a single breadth-first search per edge. Injected into a gated message-passing architecture, EGAGNN, it reaches a test MAE a factor three below the closest gated comparator on the ZINC-12k regression benchmark at 104k parameters; against bounded cycle-counting descriptors under the same architecture, it matches only a dictionary counting cycles up to length eight, using twice as many channels, while a dictionary capped at length four performs no better than no structural information at all. On graph discrimination we prove a matching limitation: on graphs where every edge sees the same number of shortest cycles of the same length, the descriptor becomes constant and any model built on it collapses back to the 1-WL bound. This holds without exception across all 400 pairs of the BREC benchmark: not one of the 90 such pairs is distinguished.

</details>


### [101] [Diffusion as a Training Curriculum for Timestep-Free Iterative Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01449)
*Mariia Drozdova,Aidan Sirbu,Pietro Miotti,Robert Obryk,Mayalen Etcheverry,Eyvind Niklasson,Blake Richards*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将扩散模型与递归推理统一：在扩散去噪器中添加持久隐藏状态并移除时间步条件，得到一个可任意深度运行的单一共享更新，成为一个"任意时刻求解器"。在Sudoku-Extreme上达到99.90%精确求解率，在Maze-Unique上达98.93%。令人惊讶的是推理时无需渐进去噪，只需每步注入高斯噪声即可几乎完美求解并收敛到稳定解；但训练时有序退火损坏仍至关重要，表明扩散的核心贡献不是推理时的采样过程，而是去噪训练课程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型与递归推理器都是迭代过程，但两者跨迭代携带信息的方式不同。作者希望融合二者优势：利用扩散模型的去噪训练机制，同时获得递归推理器可任意深度运行、随时取得更优解的特性，构建一个无需外部验证器、无需并行rollout或候选选择的统一求解框架。

Method: 1) 在扩散去噪器中引入持久隐藏状态（persistent hidden state），传递跨迭代信息；2) 移除时间步条件化，使模型变为单一共享更新，可运行至任意深度；3) 训练时保留有序退火损坏（annealed corruption）作为课程；4) 推理时无需渐进去噪，直接以最大损坏水平并使用新高斯噪声替换每个非线索变量，实现单轨迹高效搜索并收敛到稳定解。

Result: 该方法作为任意时刻求解器，推理深度远超训练展开长度和反向传播窗口时精度仍持续提升：Sudoku-Extreme精确求解率达99.90%，Maze-Unique求解率达98.93%。推理时保持最大损坏并注入新鲜高斯噪声依然能近完美求解并收敛到稳定解。

Conclusion: 扩散过程对任意时刻求解器的主要贡献不在于推理时的采样程序，而在于训练阶段作为课程的去噪训练范式。训练与推理的对称性并不必要——训练需要有序退火，推理却可简化为基础的高斯噪声注入机制，即可高效探索解空间并稳定收敛。

Abstract: Diffusion models and recursive reasoners are both iterative, but they carry information across iterations differently. We add a persistent hidden state to a diffusion denoiser and remove its timestep conditioning, leaving a single shared update that can be run to arbitrary depth. The result is an anytime solver: accuracy keeps improving with inference depth far beyond the rollout lengths and backpropagation window used in training, reaching 99.90% exact solve on Sudoku-Extreme. We also obtain 98.93% solve rate on Maze-Unique. Surprisingly, progressive denoising is unnecessary at inference: holding corruption at its maximum by replacing every non-clue variable with fresh Gaussian noise at each step retains near-perfect solving and converges to stable solutions. This simple noise-injection mechanism enables a single trajectory to efficiently explore the solution space and settle on the correct answer without parallel rollouts, candidate selection, or external verifiers required by prior reasoning models. Nonetheless, ordered annealed corruption remains critical during training, which suggests that diffusion's primary contribution to our anytime solver is not a sampling procedure at inference, but a denoising training curriculum.

</details>


### [102] [Rethinking Learnability in Offline Data-driven Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01493)
*Chao Qian,Chen-Guang Wang,Rong-Xi Tan,Ke Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出算法依赖可学习性（algorithm-dependent learnability）作为离线黑盒优化的理论框架，并基于该框架提出UGTL方法——通过轨迹学习实现高效离线优化，在Design-Bench五个任务上取得了3.1/25的聚合平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线数据驱动优化虽已涌现大量方法，但一个根本问题仍未解答：什么样的可学习性足以保证离线优化的成功？先前理论研究指出PAC可学习性不足，因为即使大部分区域学习良好，最优区域仍可能未被充分学习。这一理论空白制约了离线优化方法的设计与理解。

Method: 本文首先提出算法依赖可学习性概念，要求仅在优化器轨迹上保持精度；证明其值查询形式在离散设定（如子模最大化的贪心与局部搜索）中充分，其一阶模拟形式在凸最小化的投影梯度下降中充分。基于该概念，形式化了由轨迹构建、轨迹建模和候选生成组成的轨迹学习框架，并分析了现有方法。随后提出UGTL方法：构建反映合理搜索路径的局部连贯改进轨迹，用条件扩散模型建模，并选择多样化候选集。

Result: 在五个Design-Bench任务上，UGTL在25种方法中取得了最佳的聚合平均排名3.1/25。受控轨迹分析和跨架构替换实验证实了所提轨迹构建方案对性能提升具有重要作用。

Conclusion: 算法依赖可学习性为离线优化的理论可行性提供了更精准的条件，轨迹学习框架统一了现有方法，UGTL的实证表现证明了该理论指导下的方法在实际任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Black-Box Optimization (BBO) has found broad applications, but evolutionary algorithms and Bayesian optimization face efficiency challenges as real-world BBO problems grow increasingly complex. Data-driven optimization improves the efficiency of BBO algorithms by learning from data. Offline data-driven optimization seeks high-quality solutions using only a fixed set of previous evaluations, attracting substantial attention because it requires no additional online evaluations. Many offline optimization methods have been proposed, but a fundamental question remains unanswered: what learnability is sufficient for offline optimization? Prior theoretical studies show that Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) learnability is insufficient, as the optimal region may remain poorly learned even when most regions are well learned. In this paper, we propose algorithm-dependent learnability, which requires accuracy only on the optimizer's trajectory. We prove that its value-query form is sufficient for representative discrete settings, including greedy and local search for submodular maximization, while its first-order analogue is sufficient for projected gradient descent on convex minimization. Motivated by this notion, we formalize a trajectory-learning framework comprising trajectory construction, trajectory modeling, and candidate generation, and analyze existing trajectory-based methods under it. We further propose Uncertainty-aware Gradient-guided Trajectory Learning (UGTL), which constructs locally coherent improvement trajectories reflecting plausible search paths, models them with conditional diffusion, and selects a diverse candidate set. On five Design-Bench tasks, UGTL achieves the best aggregate mean rank, $3.1/25$, among 25 methods. Controlled trajectory analyses and cross-architecture replacements confirm that our trajectory construction plays a significant role in the improvement.

</details>


### [103] [Optimizing Byzantine Node Placement in Decentralized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01495)
*Edoardo Gabrielli,Gabriele Tolomei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出拜占庭节点放置问题作为去中心化联邦学习（DFL）安全评估中被忽视的关键维度，引入了拜占庭放置影响力（BPI）度量来量化拜占庭参与者对诚实节点的累积影响，并开发了高效优化算法来选择最具破坏性的节点放置方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DFL安全评估主要关注拜占庭参与者的行为，而忽略了哪些参与者被攻陷这一关键因素。由于聚合通过通信图分布式进行，拜占庭节点的放置位置决定了恶意影响的传播路径和范围，从而显著影响攻击效果。

Method: 将拜占庭放置形式化为对抗决策问题：在固定预算下选择最大化对诚实节点有限时间内影响的参与者集合。引入BPI度量，该度量基于实际gossip传播动力学，量化训练过程中诚实节点对拜占庭源的累积暴露。针对贪婪/非贪婪节点自交互及网络中不同节点间的多跳加权传播，设计了高效优化算法。实验覆盖六种异质图结构、无目标模型投毒和后门攻击。

Result: BPI引导的放置方案在不同网络结构下一致识别出高破坏性配置，且在通过拜占庭鲁棒聚合放宽线性gossip假设时仍然有效。相比基于节点中心性启发式的放置标准，BPI更准确地反映加权多跳传播及被攻陷节点间的交互作用。

Conclusion: 拜占庭放置是DFL威胁模型和鲁棒性评估中被严重低估的关键维度。将放置作为显式对抗决策纳入评估框架，对于全面理解DFL安全性至关重要。

Abstract: Security evaluations of decentralized federated learning (DFL) typically focus on how Byzantine participants behave, while largely overlooking which participants are compromised. Yet, because aggregation is distributed over a communication graph, the placement of Byzantine nodes determines how malicious influence propagates through the network. We therefore treat Byzantine placement as an explicit adversarial decision and formulate the attacker's objective as selecting, under a fixed compromise budget, the set of participants that maximizes its finite-time impact on honest nodes. To approximate this objective without executing the learning process for every candidate placement, we introduce Byzantine Placement Influence (BPI), a set-level measure derived from the actual gossip dynamics that quantifies the cumulative exposure of honest nodes to Byzantine sources over the training horizon. Unlike placement criteria based on node centrality heuristics, BPI directly accounts for weighted multi-hop propagation and interactions among compromised nodes. We develop efficient algorithms for optimizing BPI and evaluate them across six heterogeneous graph families, untargeted model poisoning, and backdoor attacks. BPI-guided placements consistently identify highly damaging configurations across different network structures and remain effective when the linear gossip assumption is relaxed through Byzantine-robust aggregation. Our results show that Byzantine placement is a critical but under-modeled dimension of DFL threat models and robustness evaluations.

</details>


### [104] [LatentPress: Context Compression Beyond Text and Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01507)
*Zhengze Zhou,Hejian Sang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LatentPress 提出一种新的上下文压缩范式:将对话历史与长文档压缩为连续的"记忆token"(软token),冻结的 decoder 可直接通过输入嵌入接口读取,无需推理时重建文本。仅训练一个小型 reader-matched writer(约0.1%参数量),即可实现4-16倍压缩,在 LongMemEval 上优于原始证据、文本摘要与 OCR 压缩,并展现出跨域零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的上下文压缩(文本摘要或图像渲染)默认消费者是人类,即使下游消费者是语言模型,也需要将压缩结果解码为文本或图像。这导致推理时需要重建原始表示,效率与精度均受限。作者希望探索第三种表示形态:直接让冻结 decoder 通过输入嵌入接口读取连续隐表征,从而绕过文本/视觉重建环节。

Method: LatentPress 由两部分构成:(1) 一个与 decoder reader 匹配的小型 writer 编码器,将对话历史/长文档编码为连续内存 token,这些 token 直接插入 decoder 的输入嵌入空间;(2) 训练时仅更新 writer(即一个 adapter,4.2M-26.2M 参数,约为 decoder 的 0.1%),decoder 保持冻结。writer 以 reconstruction-free 的方式端到端优化,压缩比 4-16 倍。

Result: 在 LongMemEval 上,LatentPress 以 7.70 倍压缩达到 0.504 准确率,高于未压缩证据的 0.490,显著优于文本摘要(0.184)与 OCR 压缩(0.426-0.312)。LongBench-QA 上,域内 writer 在 4-8 倍压缩时达到或超过原始上下文表现;16 倍略降。写入耗时 43ms/对话(较摘要/OCR 快约一个量级),读取比原始上下文或缓存 OCR 快 5-9 倍。两个零样本迁移实验(UItraChat→LongMemEval 记忆 QA;LongMemEval QA→LongBench 文档域)验证了接口可迁移性。

Conclusion: 直接使用连续软 token 作为机器面向的上下文接口是可行且实用的,超越了文本与视觉两种传统通道。LatentPress 证明了轻量 writer + 冻结 decoder 的组合能够在精度、压缩率与速度上同时取得优势,并为语言模型提供了一种新的原生上下文压缩表示。

Abstract: Compressed context is usually carried as human-readable text or as rendered images that must be decoded, even when its consumer is a language model. We introduce LatentPress, which writes conversational histories and long documents into a third representation: continuous memory tokens that a frozen decoder reads directly through its input-embedding interface, with no text reconstruction at inference. A small reader-matched writer compresses $4$-$16\times$ while training only an adapter (4.2M-26.2M parameters, $\sim\!0.1\%$ of the decoder). On LongMemEval, LatentPress reaches $0.504$ accuracy at $7.70\times$ compression versus $0.490$ for uncompressed evidence, outperforming text summaries (0.184) and OCR-based compression (0.426 to 0.312). On LongBench-QA, in-domain writers match or exceed raw-context reading at $4$-$8\times$ compression, while $16\times$ trails raw. Writing takes 43ms per conversation, roughly an order of magnitude faster than text summarization or OCR reconstruction, and reading is $5$-$9\times$ faster than raw context or cached OCR. We validate the interface under two transfer settings, zero-shot from UltraChat to LongMemEval memory QA and from LongMemEval-derived QA to unseen LongBench document domains, establishing direct soft tokens as a practical machine-facing context interface beyond text and vision. The implementation of the experiments could be found at: https://github.com/xuyd16ai/context_softtoken_compress .

</details>


### [105] [Quantum Sparse Autoencoders for Q-Matrix Estimation in Cognitive Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01537)
*Arif Hassan Zidan,Yi Pan,Bowen Guo,Xiang Li,Yu Bao,Yingfeng Wang,Tianming Liu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将量子机器学习（QML）应用于认知诊断中的Q矩阵估计，提出了量子稀疏自编码器（QSAE）方法。在60个模拟数据集和9个真实评估数据集上与经典自编码器（CAE）对比，发现QSAE虽未在所有模拟条件下达到最高平均精度，但在49/60个条件下方差更低、稳定性更强，并在6/9个真实数据集上表现更优，表明QML在该领域的主要优势是鲁棒性和对潜在结构复杂性的探索能力，而非普适的精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Q矩阵在认知诊断中至关重要，但数据驱动的Q矩阵估计在高相关技能和真实响应模式偏离理想生成假设时仍具挑战性。已有方法在处理此类复杂场景时存在局限，亟需探索新的估计框架。

Method: 提出量子稀疏自编码器（QSAE）：将每个学生的二元响应向量通过编码器嵌入量子电路，压缩为稀疏潜在表示，再映射到Q矩阵。该方法是QML在认知诊断中的首次应用。同时以经典自编码器（CAE）为基线，在60个模拟与9个真实数据集上进行系统基准测试。

Result: CAE在若干模拟条件下部分获得更高平均精度，但QSAE在60个条件中的49个方差更低、跨重复更稳定；在真实评估数据上，QSAE在9个数据集中有6个优于CAE。

Conclusion: QML在该场景下的核心价值并非普遍性的精度超越，而是增强的稳健性和对真实数据中潜在结构复杂性的探索能力，为认知诊断中Q矩阵估计提供了新的视角与方法路径。

Abstract: Q-matrices play a central role in cognitive diagnosis within educational data mining (EDM), specifying which latent skills each assessment item requires. Data-driven Q-matrix estimation remains challenging when assessments involve many correlated skills and when real response patterns depart from idealized generative assumptions. We introduce a novel quantum sparse autoencoder (QSAE) for Q-matrix estimation, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first application of quantum machine learning (QML) to cognitive diagnosis. Overall, the QSAE embeds each student's binary response vector into a quantum circuit using an encoder, compresses it into a sparse latent representation, and maps that representation to the Q-matrix. We benchmark the QSAE against a classical autoencoder (CAE) across 60 simulated datasets and 9 real-world assessment datasets. The results reveal complementary strengths. Although the CAE partially achieves higher average accuracy under several simulation conditions, the QSAE is substantially more stable across replications, exhibiting lower variance in 49 of the 60 conditions. Moreover, on real assessment data, the QSAE outperforms the CAE on 6 of the 9 datasets. These findings suggest that the principal advancement of QML in this setting is not universal accuracy improvement, but enhanced robustness and capability to explore latent-structure complexity in real datasets.

</details>


### [106] [NashDreamer: Model-Based Reinforcement Learning for Zero-Sum Imperfect-Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01549)
*Tomáš Holeček,Viliam Lisý*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NashDreamer是一个面向双人零和博弈不完美信息游戏的基于模型强化学习框架，提出集中式多智能体循环状态空间模型（MARSSM）来解耦环境动态与玩家策略对观测的影响，显著提升样本效率，并理论上分析了Dreamer算法家族在后验坍缩上的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型基于强化学习（MBRL）在单智能体领域取得了显著成功，但扩展到竞争性不完美信息游戏（IIGs）仍未被充分探索。多智能体环境中，对手引发的非平稳性使学习过程复杂化，而分散式模型学习面临严重的可辨识性障碍，作者认为这使得集中式模型学习在数学上是必要的。

Method: 提出NashDreamer框架，包含两个核心组件：1）集中式Multi-Agent Recurrent State-Space Model（MARSSM），将环境动态与玩家策略对其个体观测的影响进行解耦；2）设计上可与任意策略梯度算法结合使用，并在理想化模型下继承其收敛到纳什均衡的保证。

Result: 在四个基准博弈游戏的实证评估中，NashDreamer在训练早期相比无模型基线显著提升了样本效率。同时，理论分析揭示了该架构的优化景观中Dreamer算法家族在随机环境中存在后验坍缩的脆弱性。

Conclusion: NashDreamer为竞争性不完美信息游戏提供了首个具有理论保证的集中式MBRL框架，验证了集中式模型学习的必要性，但后验坍缩问题仍是未解决的开放挑战。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) has achieved remarkable results in single-agent domains, yet its extension to competitive imperfect information games (IIGs) remains underexplored. In multi-agent settings, opponent-induced non-stationarity complicates the learning process, and decentralized model learning faces severe identifiability barriers, which we argue make centralized model learning a mathematical necessity. Building on this analysis, we propose NashDreamer, a principled MBRL framework for two-player zero-sum IIGs. It introduces a centralized Multi-Agent Recurrent State-Space Model (MARSSM) that decouples environment dynamics from the effect of players' strategies on their individual observations. NashDreamer is designed to use arbitrary policy gradient algorithms and inherits their convergence guarantees towards Nash equilibria under an idealized model. Empirical evaluations across four benchmark games demonstrate that NashDreamer substantially improves sample efficiency over model-free baselines early in the training. Finally, we theoretically analyze the architecture's optimization landscape, identifying the vulnerability of the Dreamer family of algorithms to posterior collapse in stochastic environments. We leave it as an open challenge.

</details>


### [107] [A Mathematical Theory of Reusable Neural Bases for Network Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01550)
*Binshuai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出线性可复用神经基座架构（LRNBA），通过将网络块表示为共享神经基座的线性组合，在保持稳定训练的同时显著提升参数效率并降低内存成本，允许在相同参数预算下构建更宽更深的网络，实现了与传统架构相当甚至更快的收敛和更低的损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型AI模型在广泛应用中日益普及，内存成本已成为训练和推理过程中的关键瓶颈，亟需一种能够提升参数效率并降低内存开销的方法。

Method: 受RNN设计启发，将每个网络块表示为共享神经基座集合的线性组合，实现高网络压缩率同时维持稳定训练。该架构支持在相同参数预算下构建更宽和更深的网络。

Result: 大量实验表明该模型实现了与传统架构相当甚至更快的收敛速度和更低的损失，同时保持了稳定的训练动态。

Conclusion: LRNBA是一种有效的参数效率优化框架，在降低内存成本的同时保持甚至提升模型性能，为大规模AI模型在资源受限环境下的部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: As large AI models become increasingly prevalent across a wide range of applications, memory cost has become a critical bottleneck in both training and inference. To mitigate this issue, we introduce the Linear Reusable Neural Bases Architecture (LRNBA), a novel framework aimed at improving parameter efficiency and reducing memory cost. Inspired by recurrent neural network (RNN) designs, the core idea of our approach is to represent each network block as a linear combination of a shared set of neural bases, thereby enjoying highly network compression rate while maintaining stable training. The proposed architecture allows for the construction of significantly wider and deeper networks under the same parameter budget. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our model achieves comparable or even faster convergence and lower loss than classical architectures, while maintaining stable training dynamics.

</details>


### [108] [Gradient-Update Mismatch: Rethinking Conflict-Free Training of Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01558)
*Jing Xiao,Xinhai Chen,Qinglin Wang,Menghan Jia,Zhiquan Lai,Dongsheng Li,Jie Liu,Tiejun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了物理信息神经网络（PINN）训练中梯度手术方法与优化器结合时存在"梯度更新错配"（GUM）问题——即使梯度手术构造的方向无冲突，优化器的变换（如动量、自适应缩放、预条件、权重衰减）也可能破坏这一性质。作者提出梯度更新对齐（GUA）方法，将优化器更新投影回无冲突锥，并调整优化器内部状态，从而实现无冲突的最终参数更新，显著提升各类梯度手术方法的PINN训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN训练需联合优化物理残差与初边值条件损失，二者梯度常冲突。现有梯度手术方法构造无冲突方向，但忽略了优化器对此方向的后续变换可能重新引入冲突，导致冲突消除不彻底，影响训练效果。

Method: 1) 理论分析证明优化器通过历史状态、自适应缩放、预条件或解耦权重衰减等机制可将无冲突方向a_t变换为有冲突更新u_t（即GUM现象）。2) 提出GUA框架，将优化器更新u_t投影到无冲突锥C_t上得到对齐更新p_t，用p_t更新参数。3) 对含内部状态的优化器，GUA基于对齐更新重建目标并反向调整优化器内部状态，保证状态一致性。

Result: 大规模实验表明GUM在动量优化器、自适应优化器和基于曲率的优化器中普遍存在，冲突率最高达86.3%。在所有PINN测试设置中，GUA实现无冲突的参数更新，一致提升多种梯度手术方法的性能，在个别设置中相对L2误差降低高达98.2%。

Conclusion: GUM是PINN多任务训练中广泛存在但被忽视的问题，GUA通过更新层面的投影对齐有效解决该问题，为梯度手术与优化器的协同提供了新的保障，可推广至多任务学习的其他场景。

Abstract: Training Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) requires jointly optimizing physics residual and initial/boundary condition loss terms, which often induce conflicting gradients. Gradient surgery methods mitigate this issue by constructing directions from loss-specific gradients to reduce conflict before optimizer transformation. However, even when the constructed direction is conflict-free, this property may not be preserved after optimizer transformation. Let $a_t$ denote the direction constructed by gradient surgery, $u_t$ the optimizer proposal, and $\mathcal{C}_t$ the conflict-free cone induced by the loss-specific gradients. We show that modern optimizers can transform $a_t$ through mechanisms such as historical state, adaptive scaling, preconditioning, or decoupled weight decay, so $a_t \in \mathcal{C}_t$ does not generally imply $u_t \in \mathcal{C}_t$. We refer to this optimizer-induced discrepancy in conflict-freeness between $a_t$ and $u_t$ as Gradient-Update Mismatch (GUM). Accordingly, we propose Gradient-Update Alignment (GUA), which projects $u_t$ onto $\mathcal{C}_t$ to obtain the aligned update $p_t$ and applies $p_t$ to the parameters. When the optimizer maintains internal state, GUA further adjusts this state toward targets reconstructed from the applied update. We conduct extensive experiments and find that GUM is widespread across momentum, adaptive, and curvature-based optimizers, with conflict rates reaching up to 86.3%. Across all PINN settings, GUA achieves conflict-free applied updates and consistently improves various gradient surgery methods, reducing the relative $L_2$ error by up to 98.2% in individual settings. Data and code are available at https://github.com/JingXiao10/GUA.

</details>


### [109] [The Structure of Quantization Damage in LLMs: Why the Next Bit Should Be Spent Globally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01587)
*Jundong Hu,Shekar Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM后训练量化（PTQ）中精度损失的分布及如何分配额外精度预算。通过因果混合精度干预（逐层提升至8-bit并测量精度恢复量）在9个开源模型、4个架构家族上测试，发现精度损伤并非集中在任务回路、计算位置或权重统计中，而是弥散分布的（8/9模型需约一半层数才能恢复75%的差距）。在匹配的精度预算下，将预算全局用于更细的量化粒度（如group-128），比局部修复最可恢复的层更优（提升21-52点）。两个次要发现：残差受预算限制（8-bit接近无损）；峰值恢复位置与架构族内相关但跨族不相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PTQ被广泛用于降低LLM推理成本，但其精度损失不均匀且需逐模型调优。作者想知道量化损伤具体发生在哪里，以及如何最优地分配额外精度预算，以取代当前基于直觉或廉价信号（如任务回路、权重统计）的逐层优先级判断。

Method: 采用因果混合精度干预作为ground truth：将每一层逐个提升到8-bit并测量其恢复的精度。在9个模型（4个架构家族）上检验三个假设：（1）损伤集中在任务回路中；（2）集中在模型计算核心；（3）与权重统计相关。同时对比两种预算分配策略：局部修复最可恢复的层 vs 全局采用更细的量化粒度（group-128）。评估RTN、GPTQ、AWQ三种量化方法。

Result: 三个直觉假设均无法预测哪些层能从精度恢复中受益。恢复呈弥散性：8/9模型需约一半层数恢复75%的精度差距，唯Qwen3-8B为尖锐集中型。在匹配预算下，全局更细粒度（group-128）在所有8个兼容模型上优于局部修复，提升21-52点（含集中的Qwen3-8B）。次要发现：残差受预算限制（8-bit近乎无损）；峰值恢复位置在架构族内相关、跨族不相关。

Conclusion: 在此预算设置下，全局细粒度应作为优于选择性保护关键层的默认策略。更广泛地，与量化损伤相关的廉价信号并不必然能识别出恢复精度能提升准确性的层，必须通过因果干预加以验证。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is widely used to reduce the cost of serving large language models (LLMs), but its accuracy cost is uneven and is often tuned per model. We study where quantization damage occurs and how to allocate a small additional precision budget. Using causal mixed-precision intervention as ground truth (raise each layer to 8-bit in turn and measure the accuracy it recovers) across 9 open-weight models in 4 architecture families, we test 3 intuitive hypotheses: that quantization damage lives in task circuits, where the model computes, or in weight statistics. None of them predicts which layers benefit from restored precision. Recovery is instead diffuse: for 8 of 9 models, recovering 75% of the gap takes roughly half the layers; the lone exception, Qwen3-8B, is sharply concentrated. At a matched precision budget, spending it globally on finer quantization granularity beats locally repairing the most recoverable layers for all 8 group-128-compatible models (all but OpenLLaMA, whose width rules out group-128), by 21-52 points, including the concentrated Qwen3-8B. We report 2 secondary findings: the residual is budget-limited (8-bit is near-lossless in our evaluation across RTN, GPTQ, and AWQ), and the location of peak recovery correlates with architecture within a family, though not across families. Within this budget setting, global granularity is a better default than selectively protecting critical layers. More broadly, cheap signals that correlate with quantization damage do not necessarily identify where restoring precision improves accuracy; this must be tested with causal intervention.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [SilentProbe: Measuring Silent Failure in Production APIs Used as Agent Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00035)
*Zongrong Li,Shengkun Ye,Feiyou Guo,Zuoyou Dang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究调查了生产环境API中"静默失败"（合法但无匹配的查询与非法但被200响应掩盖的查询难以区分）的普遍性及其对LLM智能体的影响。通过审计2,501份OpenAPI文档和219个对27家厂商真实端点的扰动测试，发现机器可检查约束诚实报错（111/111），散文式约束静默失败（44/61，p=2e-13）。12个模型面对仅由描述举例而非全面列举的词汇时88/88全部失败，全量书写词汇时正确率达88-91%。在完整agent循环中，模型仅12%检测到静默失败，0%修复成功，41%向用户断言虚假负面结果，12%捏造数字。将词汇从描述提升到schema中修复效果显著（88/88错误降到0/89），表明一行schema变更胜过更好的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体调用生产API时无法区分"查询未匹配任何数据"与"服务器不理解查询"这两种情况——两者都返回HTTP 200和可解析的响应体，没有异常或字段供分支判断。理解是什么预测发生了哪种情况及其对智能体的影响，是构建可靠agent的迫切问题。

Method: 集成三种方法：(1) 审计721,320个参数（覆盖2,501份独立发布的OpenAPI文档），统计枚举声明率与约束编码率；(2) 通过单一聚合层Monid执行219个schema衍生的扰动，测试27家厂商的真实商业端点，记录每次调用的运行标识符；(3) 让8个家族12个模型在这些端点上执行常规任务和完整agent循环，测量静默失败的检测、修复及错误报告行为，并将约束从描述提升到schema测试修复效果。

Result: 审计发现7.5%参数声明枚举、15.2%声明任何机器可检查约束，40.1%的文档在散文而非schema中写出至少一个约束。扰动测试表明约束形式（而非厂商身份）预测诚实性：机器可检查约束在111/111中诚实报错，散文式约束在44/61中静默失败（p=2e-13）。所有模型在描述仅示例的词汇上88/88全部失败，全量列举的词汇上88-91%正确使用。完整agent循环中，模型仅12%检测到静默失败，0%修复，41%对用户断言虚假负结果，12%捏造数值。将词汇提升进schema后失败率从88/88降至0/89。

Conclusion: API文档中schema与实际约束之间的差距是静默失败的系统性根源。修复方法不在于更强的模型，而在于将散文约束正确编码为一行schema——即机器可检查的约束能显著改善LLM智能体的诚实性和可靠性。作者强调数据、代码和每次调用的运行标识符已开源，便于复现和进一步研究。

Abstract: An LLM agent calling a production API cannot distinguish a query that matched nothing from a query the server did not understand. Both return HTTP 200 with a parsable body, no exception to catch and no field to branch on. We ask what predicts which one occurred, and what it does to the agent. Auditing 721,320 parameters across 2,501 independently published OpenAPI documents, we find that 7.5% declare an enumeration and 15.2% declare any machine-checkable constraint at all, while 40.1% of documents state at least one constraint in prose that their schema does not encode. Executing 219 schema-derived perturbations against live commercial endpoints from 27 vendors, reached through a single aggregation layer (Monid) that publishes a schema and returns a run identifier for every call, we find that constraint form, not vendor identity, predicts honesty: machine-checkable constraints yielded an honest error in 111 of 111 cases, prose-only constraints failed silently in 44 of 61 (p = 2e-13). Twelve models across eight families then met these endpoints on ordinary tasks. A vocabulary that the description merely exemplifies was missed by every model on 88 of 88 attempts, while vocabularies written out in full were used correctly 88 to 91% of the time. Running the full agent loop, models detected the resulting silent failure in 12% of cases, repaired it in 0%, asserted a false negative to the user in 41%, and invented a figure in 12%. Promoting the vocabulary into the schema removes the failure, from 88 of 88 to 0 of 89. The fix is one line of schema rather than a better model. Code, schemas, perturbation sets, agent transcripts and per-call run identifiers are released at https://github.com/Jasper0122/silentprobe.

</details>


### [111] [Two-Sided State-Space Models for Sequential Recommendation with Non-Random Multimodal Review Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00165)
*Ziwen Pan,Zihan Liang,Ruoxuan Xiong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出TS-SSM，一个用于事件条件序列推荐的两侧状态空间模型。该模型同时处理用户侧和物品侧的动态演化，明确建模评论生成的"非随机缺失"模式、评论对物品状态的溢出效应（通过局部图消息传递），以及正负评论反馈的不对称影响。在多个数据集上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列推荐系统大多将评论视为被动信号仅用于更新用户状态，忽略了两个关键方面：(1) 评论生成是非随机的，依赖于用户和物品的演化潜在状态；(2) 评论不仅可以重塑物品状态，还能在相关物品间产生溢出效应并影响未来用户决策。这两个方面尚未被充分探索。

Method: TS-SSM包含三个核心组件：(1) 模态缺失非随机的融合模块，编码评论内容及信息化观察模式；(2) 用户状态演化模块，结合时间变化和局部图消息传递，利用相关物品状态细化用户偏好；(3) 物品状态演化模块，具有正负评论反馈的不对称结转效应。

Result: 在六个Amazon品类上，TS-SSM相比BSARec在Recall@20上提升14.8%-18.8%，平均超过HM4SR达11.7%。在Goodreads Fantasy数据集上，Recall@20从HM4SR的0.5191提升到0.5847。消融实验揭示了观察模式、局部传播和物品动态各自的独特贡献。

Conclusion: 通过联合建模用户与物品两侧的状态演化，并显式考虑评论生成的非随机性、评论的跨物品传播效应以及正负反馈的不对称影响，TS-SSM在事件条件序列推荐中取得了显著性能提升，验证了评论双向动态作用的重要性。

Abstract: Two-sided digital platforms are inherently dynamic: user preferences shift, item popularity evolves, and reviews both reflect and drive these changes. Yet most sequential recommendation systems treat reviews as passive signals for updating user states, leaving two aspects underexplored. First, review generation is nonrandom, depending on evolving latent states of both users and items. Second, reviews can reshape item states, induce spillover across related items, and influence future user decisions. To address these gaps, we propose a two-sided state-space model (TS-SSM) for event-conditioned sequential recommendation. TS-SSM consists of three components: (1) a modality-missing-not-at-random fusion module that encodes review content and informative observation patterns; (2) user-state evolution with temporal variation and local graph message passing that uses related item states to refine user preferences; and (3) item-state evolution with asymmetric carryover of positive and negative review feedback. In experiments across six Amazon categories, TS-SSM increases Recall@20 over BSARec by 14.8%--18.8% and exceeds HM4SR by 11.7% on average. On Goodreads Fantasy, Recall@20 improves HM4SR from .5191 to .5847. Ablations highlight distinct contributions of observation patterns, local propagation, and item dynamics.

</details>


### [112] [MUSES: A Benchmark for Prospective Intellectual-Roots Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00313)
*Rohan Pandey,Sunjae Kwon,Hong Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MUSES，一个包含233万篇论文语料、约14万条测试实例的前瞻性知识根源检索基准，并配套CiteRoots双层标注（局部引用文本的修辞层与作者背书的论文级层）。实验显示检索难度随熟悉度和功能维度下降显著（Hit@100从CiteNext的0.534降至作者认可层的0.171），一半以上的广泛层实例在K=1000时仍未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现依赖找到塑造后续工作的先例文献，但现有检索系统优化相关性与流行度，偏向中心论文而忽视那些后来被证明具有生成性的冷门工作。缺乏大规模、前瞻性的智力根源检索基准，无法有效评估系统识别真正启发性前作的能力。

Method: 构建MUSES基准：基于固定233万论文语料库，按熟悉度划分三个层级（CiteNext、CiteNew、CiteNew-Isolated），按功能维度划分广泛引用、修辞根源和作者背书根源三种标注。配套CiteRoots包括修辞层（基于局部引用文本的可扩展修辞角色层，LLM法官与人类金标准κ=0.896，含1518个来自753篇焦点论文的作者背书生成性灵感对）。在9类方法上评估，包括基于SPECTER2的简洁多质心检索器。

Result: 多质心检索器表现最强。Hit@100从CiteNext的0.534降至CiteNew的0.424、修辞性CiteNew的0.205和作者背书的CiteNew的0.171，整体下降3.1倍。在已注册的八视角全面测试审计中，约一半的广泛层实例在K=1000时仍未被解决。修辞角色与作者背书呈现显著差异（相同法官一致性κ=0.037）。

Conclusion: 现有检索系统在识别前瞻性智力根源方面表现不足，尤其是面对不熟悉文献和需要作者背书的生成性根源时性能大幅下降，揭示出修辞角色与作者背书在语义上的根本差异。发布MUSES、CiteRoots及开源法官模型以促进前瞻性检索和智力根源研究。

Abstract: Scientific discovery depends on finding prior literature that shapes what comes next. Existing retrieval systems optimize for relevance and popularity, often favoring central papers over less familiar works that later prove generative. We introduce \textbf{MUSES}, a million-instance benchmark for prospective intellectual-roots retrieval over a fixed 2.33M-paper corpus, with roughly 140K test instances per familiarity tier. To our knowledge, it is the first prospective benchmark at this scale with a shared retrieval task and author-confirmed paper-level root labels. Alongside it, \textbf{CiteRoots} pairs a scalable rhetorical layer over local citation text (LLM judge $κ= 0.896$ versus human gold) with a paper-level author-endorsed layer ($n = 1{,}518$ generative-inspiration pairs from 753 focal papers). MUSES organizes difficulty along two axes: a \emph{familiarity} axis spanning CiteNext, CiteNew, and CiteNew-Isolated, and a \emph{functional} axis spanning broad citations, rhetorical roots, and author-endorsed roots. Across 9 method classes, a lean multi-centroid retriever built on SPECTER2 is strongest. Hit@100 falls from 0.534 on CiteNext to 0.424 on CiteNew, 0.205 on rhetorical CiteNew, and 0.171 on author-endorsed CiteNew, a $3.1\times$ decline. In a registered eight-lens full-test audit, roughly half of broad-tier test instances remain unsolved at K=1{,}000. Rhetorical role and author endorsement are distinct: the same judge agrees with endorsement at $κ= 0.037$. We release MUSES, both CiteRoots layers, and a distilled open companion judge for future work on prospective retrieval and intellectual roots.

</details>


### [113] [Closed Forms and Synthetic Twins: Predicting Approximate Nearest Neighbor Recall from Embedding Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00364)
*Shmuel Herman*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文指出嵌入模型在训练和评估时假设检索是精确的，但实际生产中服务于近似索引（HNSW、IVF、PQ、FDE等），索引行为在构建前无法预测。作者提出一套"阶梯式预测工具"（基于无标签统计量）：闭式矩统计用于固定网格量化器、合成孪生语料模拟用于分区索引、尺寸外推校准孪生用于图索引，从而在构建任何索引前预测其检索召回率，预测误差在0.03以内。同时将该几何可训练化，通过优化唯一的后处理无法改变的统计量——分数边际，可同时提升所有索引族的召回率，并小幅牺牲任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入模型在生产环境中通常与近似索引（HNSW、IVF、PQ、FDE等）配合使用，但评测标准只测量精确检索，导致索引行为与编码器评测脱节——一个现代编码器通过其原始FDE索引仅能恢复14%的精确top-10结果。这种失败仅在索引构建后才暴露，而标准修补方案（如白化变换）需要随语料变化而拟合、存储和重拟合，甚至可能静默改写编码器输出。因此迫切需要一种能够在索引构建之前预测索引行为的方法。

Method: 作者提出一套"阶梯"预测工具，匹配不同索引族消耗的输入特征：(1) 对固定网格量化器（PQ、FDE）使用闭式矩统计（closed-form moment statistics）；(2) 对分区索引使用合成孪生语料（synthetic twin corpus）模拟——将聚类统计量生成化，可在其上构建并测试任何索引（包括复合生产系统）；(3) 对百万文档规模的图索引使用尺寸外推、轻量校准的孪生语料。此外，通过训练优化"分数边际"（score margin）——这一统计量是任何后处理变换都无法改变的——可同时提升所有索引族的召回率，以小幅任务代价换取召回率的整体提升。

Result: 预测结果在未见过的百万文档语料上与实测召回率的误差在0.03以内。训练分数边际可同时提升所有索引族的召回率，仅付出小幅可测的任务性能代价。该方法实现了从一次廉价测量出发，即可完成索引选择、纠错定价和生产召回率预测，适用于新语料和新索引；无需逐语料变换机制，适合持续变化的语料；并通过使编码器适应几何而非耦合于单一索引，推进了召回-计算的前沿。

Conclusion: 索引行为是可以在构建之前从原始嵌入的无标签统计量中预测的。通过在训练阶段优化分数边际这一后处理不可变的几何量，可以同时提升所有索引族的召回性能。该方法消除了对逐语料变换机制（如白化）的依赖，使得召回预测、索引选择和纠错定价在一次便宜测量中即可完成，并且让编码器与索引解耦，推动召回-计算效率前沿。

Abstract: Embedding models are trained and evaluated as if retrieval were exact; in production they serve behind approximate indexes -- HNSW, IVF, product quantization, or the fixed-dimensional encodings (FDEs) of late-interaction models -- whose behavior the encoder's benchmarks never see: one modern encoder recovers just 14% of its exact top-10 through its raw FDE index. Such failures surface only after an index is built, and the standard patches -- corpus-fitted transforms such as whitening -- must be fitted, stored, and refit as the corpus changes, and can silently rewrite what the encoder returns. This paper shows that index behavior is predictable before anything is built, from label-free statistics of the raw embeddings, through a ladder of instruments matched to what each index family consumes: (1) closed-form moment statistics for the fixed-grid quantizers (PQ, FDE); (2) simulation on a synthetic twin corpus -- cluster statistics made generative, on which any index, composed production systems included, can be built and tested -- for partition indexes; (3) size-extrapolated, lightly calibrated twins for graph indexes at million-document scale. Predictions land within 0.03 of measured recall on an unseen million-document corpus. The same geometry is trainable: targeting the one statistic no post-hoc transform can move -- the score margin -- lifts recall for every index family at once, at a small measured task cost. The result: index choice, correction pricing, and production recall forecast from one cheap measurement pass, on new corpora and new indexes alike; serving without per-corpus transform machinery, suited to continuously changing corpora; and a recall-compute frontier pushed by adapting encoders to geometry rather than coupling them to any single index.

</details>


### [114] [Towards Effective Structured Context Modeling for Conversational Recommender Systems via Dual-node Monte Carlo Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00618)
*Jincheng Zhang,Chen Huang,Wenqiang Lei,See-Kiong Ng,Yang Deng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了DREAMS，一种树形对话上下文建模框架，通过双节点类型显式捕捉多轮交互中用户偏好的演化，分别支持偏好引导（利用MCTS探索对话动作）和偏好利用（利用LLM将偏好状态转为检索查询），在基准数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话推荐系统（CRSs）需要在多轮交互中跟踪用户偏好，但现有方法对对话上下文的建模不够充分，难以显式捕捉用户偏好随时间演化的动态过程。

Method: 提出DREAMS框架，包含两种专用节点类型：(1) elicitation节点，利用蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)策略性地探索对话动作并推断潜在用户偏好；(2) exploitation节点，采用基于LLM的细化机制，将跟踪到的偏好状态转化为结构化的检索查询以进行推荐。

Result: 在基准数据集上的广泛实验表明DREAMS及其设计是有效的。

Conclusion: DREAMS通过树形对话上下文建模显式捕获用户偏好的演化，为CRS中的偏好跟踪提供了一种新颖且有效的框架方案。

Abstract: We investigate the role of conversational context modeling in user preference tracking for Conversational Recommendation Systems (CRSs). In this regard, we propose DREAMS, a novel tree-structured context modeling framework that explicitly captures user preference evolution throughout multi-turn interactions. DREAMS introduces two specialized node types to support the two fundamental objectives of CRSs: preference elicitation and preference exploitation. Specifically, elicitation nodes leverage Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) to strategically explore conversational actions and infer latent user preferences, while exploitation nodes employ LLM-based refinement to transform the tracked preference state into structured retrieval queries for recommendation. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of DREAMS and its design.

</details>


### [115] [It Takes Two to Match: Co-Evolving Generative Retriever with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00638)
*Runpeng Dai,Kaili Huang,Changsung Kang,Ciya Liao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CoGR，一个直接训练LLM在查询侧和条目侧同时构建检索表示的框架，通过生成关键词并使用倒排索引直接匹配，采用两阶段训练（监督微调+协同演化强化学习），在10个稀疏、密集和生成式基线上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的检索工作通常利用LLM进行查询侧增强（如查询扩展、数据合成），但最终匹配仍由下游检索器完成，LLM的生成能力未充分用于直接构建检索表示。同时，查询侧和条目侧的生成器缺乏协同优化，导致表示空间可能不一致。

Method: CoGR包含三个核心组件：（1）查询侧和条目侧各用一个LLM生成器，各自产出一组紧凑关键词；（2）通过倒排索引直接匹配两侧生成的关键词，兼容现有基于关键词的检索基础设施；（3）两阶段训练流程：第一阶段用监督微调建立对齐的关键词空间；第二阶段采用协同演化的强化学习，用GRPO交替优化两侧生成器——查询侧直接接收检索F1奖励，条目侧接收反事实边际奖励（衡量其生成关键词对查询侧F1的边际贡献）。两侧均优化相同的查询到条目的检索F1目标。

Result: 在内部APP市场数据集和公开WANDS基准上，CoGR在10种稀疏、密集和生成式基线中均取得最佳性能，F1相对最强基线分别提升10.9%和36.1%。进一步分析表明训练过程中query-条目关键词空间呈现稳定协同演化和持续对齐。

Conclusion: CoGR证明LLM可以直接参与双侧检索表示的构建，并通过协同演化强化学习实现查询侧与条目侧生成器的联合优化，在保持与现有关键词检索基础设施兼容的同时显著提升检索性能。

Abstract: Retrieval is the first stage of modern search and advertising systems, selecting a candidate set from a large item universe for downstream ranking and auction. Recent work increasingly leverages LLMs to improve retrieval through query expansion, data synthesis, and retrieval-feedback training. However, the generative component is typically used for query-side augmentation, while final matching is still delegated to a downstream retriever. We introduce CoGR, a retrieval framework that instead trains LLMs to directly construct retrieval representations on both query and item sides. Each generator produces a compact set of keywords, which are matched directly through an inverted index, preserving compatibility with existing keyword-based retrieval infrastructure. CoGR uses a two-stage training pipeline. Supervised fine-tuning first establishes an aligned keyword space, after which co-evolving reinforcement learning alternately optimizes the query- and item-side generators with GRPO against the opposite side's frozen index. Both sides optimize the same query-to-item retrieval $F_1$ objective: the query side receives retrieval $F_1$ directly, while the item side receives a counterfactual marginal reward measuring the change in query-side $F_1$ caused by its generated keywords. Across 10 representative sparse, dense, and generative baselines, CoGR achieves the best performance on both an internal APP Marketplace dataset and the public WANDS benchmark, improving $F_1$ over the strongest baseline by $10.9\%$ and $36.1\%$, respectively. Further analysis shows stable co-evolution and increasingly aligned query--item keyword spaces over training.

</details>


### [116] [From Saliency to Discriminability: Rank-Preserving Visual Token Pruning for VLM Rerankers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00667)
*Siyi Liu,Hanjun Yang,Chenchen Zhang,Xiaorong Zhu,Xinyu Zuo,Lisheng Duan,Haijin Liang,Jin Ma,Junfu Pu,Yongqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出RaDiCal框架，用于大规模视觉语言模型列表式重排序中的token剪枝。通过归一化注意力熵诊断注意力显著性何时可被信任，并将其与无注意力的排名判别先验融合。在多个检索基准上以20%-10%的token预算下达到或超越密集检索性能，同时减少39-45%的FLOPs并实现1.28-1.45倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模视觉语言模型用作列表式重排序器时，需要为每个查询联合处理数十个候选图像的视觉token，token剪枝对实际部署至关重要。然而现有基于注意力显著性的剪枝方法存在系统性偏差：视觉上显著的token往往捕获的是跨候选共享的顺序无关模式，与实际的排名贡献不一致。

Method: 提出基于归一化注意力熵的诊断方法（相关系数r=0.87），判断注意力显著性何时可被信任（仅当注意力集中时显著性才具有信息量）。在此基础上提出RaDiCal（Rank-Discriminative Calibration），一个免训练框架：利用归一化注意力熵决定显著性是否可信，将其与无注意力的排名判别先验融合，并从同一信任景观中选择剪枝层。

Result: 在三个检索基准和多种VLM架构上：RaDiCal在20% token预算下于Flickr30K达到与Dense MRR@10相当的性能，在MSCOCO上超过该基线；在FashionIQ上所有剪枝方法中排名第一；10%保留率下在Flickr30K和MSCOCO上保持在1.2个百分点以内。FLOPs削减39-45%，在两个VLM架构上实测加速1.28-1.45倍，且无需针对数据集重新调参。

Conclusion: RaDiCal揭示了注意力显著性在VLM列表式重排序场景中的层依赖失效问题，并提出了一种免训练、无需数据集特调的有效校准方案，在极低token预算下同时兼顾了排名性能与计算效率。

Abstract: Large vision-language models used as listwise rerankers must jointly process visual tokens from tens of candidates per query, making token pruning essential for practical deployment. Existing pruning methods retain tokens by attention saliency, yet we show that saliency is systematically misaligned with ranking contribution: visually prominent tokens often capture order-neutral patterns shared across candidates. This mismatch is layer-dependent: saliency becomes informative only where attention is concentrated, and normalized attention entropy diagnoses the reliability shift (Pearson r=0.87). We propose RaDiCal (Rank-Discriminative Calibration), a training-free framework that uses normalized attention entropy to decide when saliency can be trusted, fusing it with an attention-free rank-discriminative prior and selecting pruning layers from the same trust landscape. Across three retrieval benchmarks and multiple VLM architectures, RaDiCal matches Dense MRR@10 on Flickr30K and surpasses it on MSCOCO at a 20% token budget, ranks first among all pruning methods on FashionIQ, and holds within 1.2 pp on Flickr30K and MSCOCO at 10% retention. It cuts FLOPs by 39--45% and delivers 1.28--1.45$\times$ measured speedups across two VLM architectures without dataset-specific retuning.

</details>


### [117] [SwapRec: Warming Up Cold Items Through Training-Time Swaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00913)
*Marta Moscati,Jan Malte Lichtenberg,Davide Abbattista,Antonio De Candia,Laura Boggia,Matteo Ruffini*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SwapRec是一种解决ID-based推荐系统中冷启动物品交互问题的训练时方法，通过在训练阶段应用与推理阶段相同的"交换"启发式（将冷物品替换为相似的热邻居），使序列推荐模型对冷物品交互更具鲁棒性，从而在保持冷物品推荐比例的同时提升推荐精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实工业场景中，冷物品交互会降低用户偏好估计的准确性；若排除冷物品则无法进行实时推荐更新。常见的推理时启发式做法是将冷物品替换为最相似的"热"邻居，但这会破坏序列模型的偏好估计，导致推荐性能下降。现有方法缺乏对这一问题的系统研究。

Method: SwapRec的核心思想是将推理阶段使用的交换启发式策略同步引入训练阶段，即在进行模型训练时，也按照物品侧信息相似度将冷物品替换为其最相似的热物品邻居，从而使序列推荐模型（如SASRec等SOTA架构）在训练阶段就学习到对这类交换操作的鲁棒表示。

Result: 在三个推荐领域（在线购物、电影、音乐）的定量实验中，SwapRec均显著提升了在冷物品交互存在情况下的推荐准确性，同时推荐列表中的冷物品比例也更高，且该方法的有效性不依赖于底层具体的序列推荐架构。

Conclusion: SwapRec是一种简单易实现且与具体模型架构无关的训练策略，能够有效缓解ID-based序列推荐系统在处理冷物品交互时推荐精度下降的问题，在精度和冷物品覆盖率之间取得了双赢效果。

Abstract: Interactions with cold items negatively impact real-time personalization of ID-based recommender systems. This is because the use of such interactions degrades user preference estimates, whereas excluding cold items from the user profile prevents real-time recommendation updates. In industrial scenarios, one heuristic often applied to address this shortcoming at inference time is to replace, i.e., "swap", cold-start items by their most similar "warm" neighbor, where similarity is inferred from the items' side information. In this paper, we demonstrate that sequential models, most often used for real-time personalization, are not robust to such swaps, and propose SwapRec, an approach to address this issue. SwapRec relies on using the same swap heuristics already at training time. We apply SwapRec to state-of-the-art models for sequential recommendation and analyze its impact by means of quantitative experiments in three recommendation domains (online shopping, movie, music). The experimental results show that, irrespective of the underlying sequential architecture, our easy-to-implement SwapRec approach allows for substantially more accurate recommendations when in presence of interactions with cold items, simultaneously leading to a larger percentage of cold items in the recommendation lists.

</details>


### [118] [TGR: Advancing Industrial Recommendation from Generative-Paradigm Ranking toward Unified Generation and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00986)
*TGR Team,Lei Cheng,Haonan Hu,Beibei Kong,Yudong Li,Zang Li,Yunsheng Pang,Hongyang Su,Jianchao Tu,Yunlong Wang,Bing Wen,Junzhang Zhu,Shaojie Zhu,Chengxiang Zhuo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 腾讯提出TGR（Tencent Generative Recommendation），一个将工业推荐系统推向生成式范式的框架，包含三个耦合方向：TGR-GenRank基于CCFormer架构升级排序、TGR-GenRec探索两种端到端生成范式（BARGE与HiGR）、TGR-Reason注入离线生成的推理标记以增强在线推理能力。该系统已部署于腾讯多个生产场景服务数亿用户，各模块在多场景中均取得显著收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统级联式推荐系统（召回-粗排-精排-重排）各阶段独立优化，存在三大局限：模型难以规模化扩展、决策碎片化缺乏全局一致性、缺乏语义知识与推理能力。为突破这些瓶颈，作者提出向生成式推荐范式演进。

Method: TGR沿三个方向推进：1) TGR-GenRank：通过CCFormer统一特征tokenization、可扩展Transformer骨干、特征域分离交叉注意力、子空间token混合和层级序列压缩来升级排序，同时保留逐物品多任务输出；2) TGR-GenRec：探索两种端到端生成范式——BARGE通过物品上下文感知注意力、层级路径重排和正交双路径解码来解决层级语义ID生成中的物品边界损失和语义漂移问题；HiGR通过前缀结构语义ID、粗到细解码和列表级多目标对齐实现整l条slate生成；3) TGR-Reason：将离线生成的语义ID推理token注入在线解码过程，无需请求时rollout即可提供推理能力。

Result: CCFormer在5个A/B测试场景中获得显著提升，其中2个已全量上线，带来+3.57% CTR和+1.71%广告收入提升；BARGE将Hit@5提升10.2-16.9%，全量上线后CTR提升+0.60%、阅读时长提升+1.70%；HiGR将离线slate质量提升15.9-21.3%并获得5倍推理加速，线上观看时长最高提升+1.22%、视频浏览量提升+1.73%；TGR-Reason将冷启动新用户Hit@1提升477.8%，线上有效消费+1.75%、新用户曝光到转化提升+13.09%。

Conclusion: TGR验证了生成式范式的工程可行性与商业价值，成功部署于腾讯覆盖数亿用户的生产系统中。语义ID推理token的引入使推荐系统具备离线注入的推理能力，证明了从判别式/级联式推荐向端到端生成式推荐的整体演进路径不仅可行且能带来稳定的业务收益。

Abstract: Industrial recommender systems typically rely on cascaded retrieval, pre-ranking, ranking, and reranking stages, whose separately optimized models limit scaling, fragment decision making, and lack semantic knowledge and reasoning. We present TGR (Tencent Generative Recommendation), an industrial framework that advances recommendation toward the generative paradigm along three coupled directions. TGR-GenRank upgrades ranking through CCFormer, which combines unified feature tokenization, a scalable Transformer backbone, feature-field separated cross attention, subspace token mixing, and hierarchical sequence compression while retaining per-item multi-task outputs. TGR-GenRec explores end-to-end generation under two paradigms: BARGE bridges item-boundary loss and semantic drift in hierarchical semantic-ID generation through item context-aware attention, hierarchical path reranking, and orthogonal dual-path decoding; HiGR performs whole-slate generation with prefix-structured semantic IDs, coarse-to-fine decoding, and listwise multi-objective alignment. TGR-Reason injects offline-generated semantic-ID reason tokens into online decoding, providing reasoning without request-time rollout. TGR is deployed across Tencent production surfaces serving hundreds of millions of users. CCFormer delivers significant gains in five A/B-tested scenarios and is fully launched in two, including +3.57% CTR and +1.71% advertising revenue. BARGE improves Hit@5 by 10.2-16.9% and yields +0.60% CTR and +1.70% reading time after full rollout. HiGR improves offline slate quality by 15.9-21.3% with a 5x inference speedup and achieves up to +1.22% watch time and +1.73% video views. TGR-Reason raises cold-start new-user Hit@1 by 477.8% and delivers +1.75% effective consumption and +13.09% new-user exposure-to-conversion online.

</details>


### [119] [Web Price Extraction: State of the Art and an Adaptive Browserless Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01030)
*Evgeniia Kositsyna,Jorge Lloret-Gazo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应的无浏览器（browserless）价格提取系统，通过贝叶斯动态规则权重更新和遗传算法全局参数优化，将精度从77.2%提升至87.3%，并将每页平均处理时间降低约14%，在低计算成本下实现高提取精度，成为手动规则方案与资源密集型浏览器/LLM方案之间的有竞争力替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网站价格提取是电商市场监测、比价和商业分析的关键任务。现有四类方法各有权衡：手工包装器/规则归纳精度高但对结构变化适应差且维护成本高；基于浏览器的方法能处理动态内容但计算资源消耗大、扩展性差；无浏览器方法速度快成本低但规则依赖特定站点；机器学习/LLM方法适应性强但需要训练数据和大量计算。作者希望构建一个兼具快速低成本与跨站点鲁棒性的自适应无浏览器价格提取系统。

Method: 作者实现了一个基线架构，将HTML页面分片与句法、语义和频率规则相结合；在此基础上进行两项扩展：（1）采用贝叶斯方法动态更新规则权重；（2）使用遗传算法优化系统的全局参数。两者构成混合方案，在不依赖浏览器渲染的情况下提升对不同网站结构的鲁棒性。

Result: 混合方案将精度从77.2%提升至87.3%，并将每页平均处理时间相对基线降低约14%。实验证实该系统是手动调优的无浏览器方案以及资源密集型的浏览器或LLM方案的竞争性替代选择，能够在低计算成本下实现高提取精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应无浏览器价格提取系统在精度与可扩展性之间取得了良好平衡，证明了结合贝叶斯权重更新和遗传算法参数优化的混合方案是实际电商价格提取场景中兼顾精度与效率的可行路线。

Abstract: Price extraction from websites is a key task for market monitoring, price comparison, and business analytics in e-commerce. Existing approaches can be broadly divided into four groups, and understanding their trade-offs in accuracy and scalability is essential for selecting suitable extraction strategies. Classical methods rely on manually written wrappers and rule induction from labeled pages, offering high accuracy but adapting poorly to structural changes and requiring considerable maintenance effort. Browser-based methods, using tools such as Selenium and Puppeteer, handle dynamic JavaScript content but consume large computational resources and scale poorly. Browserless approaches retrieve HTML directly via HTTP requests, offering significant gains in speed and cost, but rely on rules calibrated for specific sites. Methods based on machine learning and large language models offer adaptability but require training data and substantial computation.
  Our main contribution is an adaptive browserless price extraction system that improves robustness to structural differences between websites. We implemented a baseline architecture combining HTML page fragmentation with syntactic, semantic, and frequency rules, and extended it in two ways: a Bayesian approach that dynamically updates rule weights, and a genetic algorithm that optimizes the system's global parameters. This hybrid scheme increased precision from 77.2% to 87.3% and reduced average per-page processing time by approximately 14% relative to the baseline, confirming it as a competitive alternative to manually tuned browserless solutions and to more resource-intensive browser- or LLM-based methods, offering high extraction accuracy at low computational cost.

</details>


### [120] [World Model-Guided Reinforcement Learning via Counterfactual User Engagement Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01067)
*Ang Li,Xin Xu,Bin Liang,Yue Ma,Fubang Zhao,Yangyang Kang,Kam-Fai Wong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出WMG-RL框架，通过一个冻结的用户模拟器（UEWM）在真实用户曝光前提供奖励监督信号，在不收集在线反馈的情况下训练强化学习推荐策略，使1.7B的学生策略在推荐任务上匹配甚至超越更大规模的LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以用户为中心的强化学习代理受限于在线反馈收集的成本、延迟和风险，同时缺乏在同一用户状态下的反事实对比。这阻碍了策略优化的效率与安全性，亟需一种无需真实在线交互即可提供奖励信号的方法。

Method: 提出WMG-RL框架：1）构建User Engagement World Model（UEWM）作为冻结的用户模拟器，将推荐项视为动作、用户异质反馈视为环境观测；UEWM从用户参与历史中推断用户特定动态并应用于候选项；2）下游策略为同一历史提出多个候选项，UEWM并行预测对应参与反馈；3）模拟反馈被转换为稠密奖励用于策略优化。

Result: 实验表明UEWM能跨领域提供可靠且可迁移的奖励信号，WMG-RL使1.7B的紧凑学生策略在推荐任务上匹配甚至超越更大的LLM。

Conclusion: 基于世界模型的用户模拟可作为反事实奖励来源，有效替代在线真实反馈，实现低成本、低延迟、低风险的用户中心化策略优化，同时验证了小型模型可以通过该框架获得超越大规模模型的推荐能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for user-centric agents is limited by the cost, latency, and risk of collecting online feedback, as well as by the lack of counterfactual comparisons under the same user state. In this paper, we propose World Model-Guided Reinforcement Learning via counterfactual user engagement simulation (WMG-RL), a framework in which a frozen user simulator provides reward supervision before real user exposure. Motivated by language world models, we instantiate the simulator as a User Engagement World Model (UEWM), which treats a recommended item as the agent action and the user's heterogeneous feedback as the environment observation. Rather than learning one fixed environment transition, UEWM learns to infer user-specific dynamics from engagement history and apply them to candidate items. In WMG-RL, a downstream policy proposes multiple candidate items for the same history; UEWM predicts the corresponding engagement feedback in parallel; and the simulated feedback is converted into dense rewards for policy optimization. Experiments show that UEWM provides reliable and transferable reward signals across domains, and that WMG-RL enables a compact 1.7B student policy to match or surpass much larger LLMs on downstream recommendation tasks.

</details>


### [121] [From Language to Behavior: Scaling Sequence Transformers for Industrial Recommendation Ranking with Rec-Native Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01240)
*Jie Chen,Xiangqian Yu,Yanchao Lian,Tan Lu,Run Yang,Zhengchun Shang,Xing Wang,Cheng Chen,Ke Hu,Qiang Li,Tianjiu Yin,Xiaobing Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了ReST，一个面向生产环境推荐排序的Transformer扩展框架，通过解决行为序列信号质量差和计算不对称两大挑战，实现更高的精度和更一致的扩展性，并在工业环境中取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缩放Transformer在语言建模中取得了巨大成功，但将其直接迁移到生产推荐中的行为序列建模面临两大核心挑战：(1) 信号质量差异——推荐中的行为序列噪声大、时间不规则、监督稀疏；(2) 计算不对称——每次请求需要在严格的延迟预算内基于共享用户历史对众多候选进行打分。

Method: ReST是一个推荐原生的Transformer扩展框架，包含两大方面：(1) 针对信号质量，设计了带双门控注意力的序列编码器、旋转位置和时间嵌入、稳定的残差归一化以及仅训练阶段的辅助目标；(2) 针对计算不对称，将排序分解为重型的可复用编码器和轻量级交叉解码器，采用无投影的KV注意力与token特定参数化，并耦合用户级共享前缀训练与服务，实现"一次计算、多次解码"的排序方式。

Result: 在工业和公开基准上，ReST实现了更高的精度，并在序列长度、深度和宽度维度上比LLM风格的Transformer块更一致地扩展（后者在这些维度上会饱和）。在广告平台生产环境的一周在线A/B测试中，ReST在50ms P99预算内将在线AUC提升1.31%，并将核心收入指标提升11.93%。ReST此后已全面部署到生产环境中。

Conclusion: ReST证明了行为序列缩放是生产推荐排序中一个尚未被充分利用但有前景的方向，通过针对推荐特有的信号质量和计算不对称问题设计原生Transformer框架，可以在生产环境中实现精度和收益的显著提升，并已成功完成全面部署。

Abstract: Scaling Transformers has driven large gains in language modeling, but transplanting this to behavior-sequence modeling in production ranking is challenging: recommendation differs in signal quality, where behavior sequences are noisy, temporally irregular, and sparsely supervised, and in computation asymmetry, where each request scores many candidates against one shared user history under tight latency budgets. We propose ReST, a recommendation-native Transformer scaling framework. For signal quality, it introduces a sequence encoder with dual-gated attention, rotary positional and temporal embedding, stabilized residual normalization, and training-only auxiliary objectives. For computation asymmetry, it factorizes ranking into a heavy reusable encoder and a lightweight cross decoder with projection-free KV attention and token-specific parameterization, coupling user-level shared-prefix training with shared-prefix serving for compute-once, decode-many-times ranking. Across industrial and public benchmarks, ReST achieves higher accuracy and scales more consistently along sequence length, depth, and width, where LLM-style Transformer blocks saturate. A one-week online A/B test on a production advertising platform improves online AUC by 1.31% and lifts a core revenue metric by 11.93% within a 50 ms P99 budget; ReST has since been fully deployed in production, showing that behavior-sequence scaling remains a promising, under-exploited axis for production ranking.

</details>


### [122] [MIDR: Enrichment-Augmented Indexing for Multimodal Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01316)
*Debanjan Mahata,Atharva Tendle,Daniel Preotiuc-Pietro,Yong Zhuang,Ozan Irsoy*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MIDR (Multimodal Indexing for Document Retrieval)，一种无需训练的框架，在索引阶段利用多模态LLM将视觉丰富文档（含表格、图表、版式等）转换为验证过的文本字段，结合BM25F与稠密检索进行检索，避免在查询时使用昂贵的视觉late-interaction推理。在ViDoRe V3上，MIDR Hybrid在英语域平均nDCG达0.6219，较BM25相对提升23%；在法语文档域将BM25从0.1532提升至0.5448 nDCG并超越ColQwen2.5；在七个域中四个域领先ColQwen2.5，同时索引内存约小9倍、查询延迟约低2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉丰富文档的检索面临表征困境：表格、图表、图片和版式关系等重要内容在传统OCR线性化过程中会被破坏或丢失。ColPali系列通过patch级多向量索引和late-interaction评分解决此问题，但将视觉推理保留在查询时延路径上，部署成本高。作者希望将多模态推理从查询时迁移到索引时，提供一种更高效的准确率-部署权衡方案。

Method: MIDR在文档摄取阶段使用多模态LLM将渲染的页面转换为经过验证（verified）的文本字段，这些字段使用BM25F索引，并可选地与稠密检索融合形成混合检索系统。该方法实现文本中心的serving（无需视觉模型参与查询路径），同时保留多模态grounded的索引证据。

Result: 在ViDoRe V3上：MIDR Hybrid在五个英文域平均nDCG达到0.6219，较BM25相对提升23.0%，与ColQwen2.5竞争可比；在两个法语文档域，enrichment使英文查询与法文页面文本之间的检索桥接，将BM25从0.1532提升到0.5448 nDCG，超过ColQwen2.5；在全部七个域中，MIDR在四个域领先ColQwen2.5，同时索引内存约小9倍（约2x lower query latency）。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，将多模态推理从serving-time的视觉late-interaction迁移到index-time，能够在保持竞争性甚至更优检索精度的同时，大幅降低部署成本（索引内存和查询延迟），为视觉丰富文档检索提供了更有说服力的准确率-部署替代方案。

Abstract: Retrieval over visually rich documents has a representation problem: important content often lives in tables, charts, figures, and layout relations that plain OCR linearizes, corrupts, or omits. ColPali-family visual retrievers address this with patch-level multi-vector indexes and late-interaction scoring, keeping image-derived retrieval on the query-time serving path. We introduce MIDR (Multimodal Indexing for Document Retrieval), a training-free framework for enrichment-augmented indexing that shifts multimodal reasoning to index time. During ingestion, a multimodal LLM converts rendered pages into verified textual fields that are indexed with BM25F and optionally fused with dense retrieval, enabling text-centric serving over multimodally grounded evidence. On ViDoRe V3, MIDR Hybrid achieves 0.6219 average nDCG across five English domains, a 23.0% relative gain over BM25, remaining competitive with ColQwen2.5. On two French-document domains, enrichment bridges English queries and French page text, lifting BM25 from 0.1532 to 0.5448 nDCG and outperforming ColQwen2.5. Across all seven domains, MIDR leads ColQwen2.5 on four while using approximately 9x smaller index memory and approximately 2x lower query latency. These results establish index-time multimodal reasoning as a compelling accuracy-deployment alternative to serving-time visual late interaction.

</details>
